2. Maximum Spectacle Blind size shall be 12NPS. A blind and spacer shall be used for larger pipe sizes. Spectacle Blind material shall be A516 Gr 70N. For low temperature service, material shall be Charpy Y-notch tested at temperatures no warmer than the minimum allowable operating temperature.
4. See Owner's Specification GS 2.10 for Nondestructive Examination requirements. When the amount of radiographic examination is less than 100%, the progressive examination requirements of B31.3 Clause 341.3.4 or CSA Z662 Clause 7.10.3.1 shall apply.

16. For glycol and hot oil service, use SW fittings, except valves which may be threaded.
18. A53 Gr. B Pipe may be substituted with approval. (ERW for NPS 12-18, DSAW for NPS 20-24). Check wall thickness.
24. If using SW flanges, bore to match pipe.
26. For FF Flanges use Class 150 - 3 mm THK Full Face Gaskets, "Klinger® 1000", "Durlon-8600" or "Garlock 3150".
28. RFSO flanges may be substituted with approval.
29. For treated de-ironized boiler feed water upstream of the deaerator, use 304L stainless steel pipe and fittings with 316 stainless steel valves.
31. Use Flat Faced (FF) flanges in firewater service or when mating to cast iron flanges.

3. Buried pipe to be externally coated with materials suitable for the application.
4. See Owner's Specification GS 2.10 for Nondestructive Examination requirements. When the amount of radiographic examination is less than 100%, the progressive examination requirements of B31.3 Clause 341.3.4 or CSA Z662 Clause 7.10.3.1 shall apply.
5. Full port valves for use on lines subject to pigging operations.
8. Lines subject to pigging operations to be 3 NPS or larger.
9. Design Factors per CSA Z662 equation 4.3.5.1 are F=0.8, L=0.625, J=1, T=1. Designer shall verify design factors are appropriate. Re-evaluate wall thickness, if required.
24. If using SW flanges, bore to match pipe.
27. Special attention may be required for internal corrosion in fresh or produced water service.

23. Consider maximum pressure of 1035 kPag to be consistent with category D fluid service per B31.3 and use reduced testing requirements.

49. No welding of galvanized pipe.

**NOTES:**

1. Maximum Spectacle Blind size shall be 12NPS. A blind and spacer shall be used for larger pipe sizes. Spectacle Blind material shall be A516 Gr.70N. For low temperature service, material shall be Charpy V-notch tested at temperatures no warmer than the minimum metal design temperature.
2. Butt weld pipe to be externally coated with materials suitable for the application.
3. See Owner's Specification GS 2.10 for Nondestructive Examination requirements. When the amount of radiographic examination is less than 100%, the progressive examination requirements of B31.3 Clause 341.3.4 or CSA Z662 Clause 7.10.3.1 shall apply.
4. Maximum allowable temperature 100°C.
5. Full port valves for use on lines subject to pigging operations.
6. Lines subject to pigging operations to be 3 NPS or larger.
7. Design Factors for CSA Z662 equation 4.3.1.1 are: F=0.8, L=0.925, J=1, T=1. Designer shall verify design factors are appropriate. Re-evaluate wall thickness, if required.
8. For glycol and hot oil service, use SW fittings, except valves which may be threaded.
9. A53 Gr. B Pipe may be substituted with approval. (ERW for NPS 12-18, DSAW for NPS 20-24). Check wall thickness.
10. If using SW flanges, bore to match pipe.
11. Maximum allowable pressure for 3 and 4 NPS threaded piping is 3500 kPag; service must be non-cyclic and fluid must be low vapour pressure.

**NOTES:**

1. See Sour Service Definition and Material Requirements in Owner's Specification GS 1.0.
2. Maximum Spectacle Blind size shall be 12NPS. A blind and spacer shall be used for larger pipe sizes. Spectacle Blind material shall be A516 Gr 70N. For low temperature service, material shall be Charpy V-notch tested at temperatures no warmer than the minimum metal design temperature.
4. Buried pipe to be externally coated with materials suitable for the application.
6. Full port valves for use on lines subject to pigging operations.
7. Threaded connections allowed subject to compliance with Owner's Specification 2.1 Piping Design.
9. Design Factors for CSA Z662 equation 4.3.5.1 are F=0.8, L=0.625, J=1, T=1. Designer shall verify design factors are appropriate. Re-evaluate wall thickness, if required.
10. Flanged valves 1/2 – 1 1/2 NPS for use on Post Weld Heat Treated lines.
12. PWHT required when service conditions of ISO 15156 are met except as exempted in Owner's Specification GS 2.0 Welding Appendix D. When sour service piping is exempted from PWHT, it shall be indicated on drawings provided to the piping fabricator.
14. Dual certified materials may be used A234 WPB/Z245.11 SS, A106 Gr B/Z245.11 Cat I SS, A105/Z245.12 SS. Fittings may have only ASTM specification only when suitable CSA Z245 materials not available.
15. No threaded connections allowed as primary process connections.
24. If using SW flanges, bore to match pipe.
32. Material conformance to CSA Z245.15 SS required.

**NOTES:**

1. See Sour Service Definition and Material Requirements in Owner's Specification GS 1.0
2. Maximum Spectacle Blind size shall be 12NPS. A blind and spacer shall be used for larger pipe sizes. Spectacle Blind material shall be A516 Gr 70N. For low temperature service, material shall be Charpy V-notch tested at temperatures no warmer than the minimum metal design temperature.
6. Full port valves for use on lines subject to pigging operations.
7. Threaded connections allowed subject to compliance with Owner's Specifications GS 2.1 Piping Design.
8. Lines subject to pigging operations to be 3 NPS or larger.
9. Design Factors for CSA Z662 Section 4.3.5.1 are F=0.8, L=0.625, J=1, T=1. Designer shall verify design factors are appropriate. Re-evaluate wall thickness, if required.
10. PWHT required when service conditions of ISO 15156 are met except as exempted in Owner's Specification GS 2.0 Welding
15. Appendix D. When sour service piping is exempted from PWHT, it shall be indicated on drawings provided to the piping fabricator.
16. No insulated connections allowed as primary process connections.
22. Dual certified materials may be used A333 Gr. 6 / Z245.1 Gr. 241 Cal I SS, A420 WPL 6 / Z245.11 Gr. 241 Cal I SS, A350 LF2 / Z245.12 Cal I SS. Fittings may have only ASTM specification only when suitable CSA Z245 materials not available.
32. Material conformance to CSA Z245.15 SS required.

## NOTES:

1. See Sour Service Definition and Material Requirements in Owner's Specification GS 1.0.
2. Buried pipe to be externally coated with materials suitable for the application.
3. Full port valves for use on lines subject to pigging operations.
4. Threaded connections selected subject to compliance with Owner's Specifications GS 2.1 Piping Design.
8. Lines subject to pigging operations to be 3 NPS or larger.
9. Design Factors for CSA Z662 (clause 4.3.5.1 are: F=0.8, L=0.625, J=1, T=1. Designer shall verify design factors are appropriate. Re-evaluate wall thickness, if required.
10. PWHT required when service conditions of ISO 15156 are met except as exempted in Owner's Specification GS 2.9 Welding Appendix D. When sour service piping is exempted from PWHT, it shall be indicated on drawings provided to the piping fabricator.
15. No threaded connections allowed as primary process connections.
32. Material conformance to CSA Z245.15 SS required.
33. Dual certified materials may be used A333 Gr. 6 / Z245.1 Gr. 241 Cat II (M45C) SS, A420 WPL-6 / Z245.11 Gr. 241 Cat II (M45C) SS, A350 LF2 / Z245.12 Cat II (M45C) SS - Fittings may have only ASTM specification only when suitable CSA Z245 material not available.
